| `clm_a8c5d541c698_ollama_intern_1` | `04-environment-reproducibility` | overgeneralization_finding | overgeneralized_claim (warn): Claim clm_a8c5d541c698_ollama_intern_1 states that ComfyUI runs in a separate Python environment regardless of the version used, but the source only supports this claim for frequent updates to ComfyUI. | `audits/04-environment-reproducibility-findings.jsonl` |
| `clm_a8c5d541c698_ollama_intern_12` | `04-environment-reproducibility` | overgeneralization_finding | overgeneralized_claim (warn): Claim clm_a8c5d541c698_ollama_intern_12 states users are directed to install PyTorch version specific to their hardware, but the source only directs users to 'install PyTorch (version specific to your hardware)' without specifying why. | `audits/04-environment-reproducibility-findings.jsonl` |
| `clm_b81c03003037_ollama_intern_11` | `04-environment-reproducibility` | overgeneralization_finding | overgeneralized_claim (warn): The claim that 'ComfyUI Desktop uses automatic detection updates' overgeneralizes the evidence, which only supports the use of automatic updates for ComfyUI itself. | `audits/04-environment-reproducibility-findings.jsonl` |
| `clm_2a30a3ff7502_ollama_intern_1` | `04-environment-reproducibility` | overgeneralization_finding | overgeneralized_claim (warn): The claim that 'ComfyUI Portable Windows is a self-hosted local installation guide' is not supported by the evidence, which only states it is a portable version. | `audits/04-environment-reproducibility-findings.jsonl` |
| `clm_2a30a3ff7502_ollama_intern_12` | `04-environment-reproducibility` | overgeneralization_finding | overgeneralized_claim (warn): The claim that 'ComfyUI Portable provides update scripts for Windows' overgeneralizes, as the evidence only shows scripts to update ComfyUI itself. | `audits/04-environment-reproducibility-findings.jsonl` |
| `clm_f9b159fab5ef_ollama_intern_1` | `04-environment-reproducibility` | overgeneralization_finding | overgeneralized_claim (warn): Claim clm_f9b159fab5ef_ollama_intern_1 recommends using ComfyUI Manager for custom nodes, but the source only mentions it as 'Recommended'. | `audits/04-environment-reproducibility-findings.jsonl` |
| `clm_aa570bcb9060_ollama_intern_18` | `04-environment-reproducibility` | overgeneralization_finding | overgeneralized_claim (warn): The claim that some modules may need time to support NumPy 2 overgeneralizes the source, which only suggests that 'some modules' may require rebuilding. | `audits/04-environment-reproducibility-findings.jsonl` |
| `clm_aa570bcb9060_ollama_intern_26` | `04-environment-reproducibility` | overgeneralization_finding | overgeneralized_claim (warn): The claim that ComfyUI's 'execute' function failed again due to numpy not being available overgeneralizes the source, which only cites a single instance of this error. | `audits/04-environment-reproducibility-findings.jsonl` |
| `clm_aa570bcb9060_ollama_intern_31` | `04-environment-reproducibility` | overgeneralization_finding | overgeneralized_claim (warn): The claim that updates to ComfyUI can break the environment due to heavy libraries like torch overgeneralizes the source, which only mentions GPU dependencies. | `audits/04-environment-reproducibility-findings.jsonl` |
| `clm_2086dbbb6560_ollama_intern_49` | `04-environment-reproducibility` | overgeneralization_finding | overgeneralized_claim (warn): The claim that the --cuda-device startup argument now accepts a string allowing multiple device IDs to be provided overgeneralizes, as it only applies when using the refactored version of the argument. | `audits/04-environment-reproducibility-findings.jsonl` |
| `clm_a8c5d541c698_ollama_intern_12` | `04-environment-reproducibility` | overgeneralization_finding | scope_widening (warn): The assertion that users are directed to install PyTorch version specific to their hardware, but the scope is only about installation instructions, which seems too broad. | `audits/04-environment-reproducibility-findings.jsonl` |
| `clm_fcdd7f1c90e9_ollama_intern_8` | `04-environment-reproducibility` | overgeneralization_finding | scope_widening (warn): The claim about LD_LIBRARY_PATH errors in ComfyUI being due to PyTorch library path issues uses universal quantifiers but the scope is only when encountering these specific errors. | `audits/04-environment-reproducibility-findings.jsonl` |
| `clm_aa570bcb9060_ollama_intern_31` | `04-environment-reproducibility` | overgeneralization_finding | scope_widening (block): The claim that updates to ComfyUI can break the environment due to heavy libraries like torch implies a general issue, but the evidence specifically mentions GPU dependencies. | `audits/04-environment-reproducibility-findings.jsonl` |
